Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any algebraic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 47. The projective resolution has length at most 581 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebraic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 47 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 581. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the algebraic structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 953. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 296 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on algebraic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 953. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 296. QED.
